(4) pac‍cuppan‍nātītavārau

(ka) anulaumapuggalau

335. (ka) yassa  cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa sautindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti? āmantā|

(kha) yassa vā pana sautindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ acakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ sautindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| sacakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ sautindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa  cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti? āmantā|

(kha) yassa vā pana ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa  cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ acakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| sacakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ ghānindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

yassa cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa itthindriyaṃ…pai॰… purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti? āmantā|

yassa vā pana purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ acakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| sacakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ purisindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti? āmantā|

(kha) yassa vā pana jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ acakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| sacakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ jīvitindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

yassa cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa saumanassindriyaṃ…pai॰… upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti? āmantā|

yassa vā pana upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ acakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau  ca taisaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| sacakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ upaikkhindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

yassa  cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ…pai॰… manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti? āmantā|

yassa vā pana manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ acakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| sacakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ manindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati| (cakkhundriyamūlakaṃ)

336. yassa ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa itthindriyaṃ…pai॰… purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti? āmantā|

yassa vā pana purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ aghānakānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| saghānakānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ purisindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha ghānindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti? āmantā|

(kha) yassa vā pana jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ aghānakānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| saghānakānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ jīvitindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha ghānindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

yassa  ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa saumanassindriyaṃ…pai॰… upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti? āmantā|

yassa  vā pana upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ aghānakānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| saghānakānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ upaikkhindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha ghānindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

yassa ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ…pai॰… manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti? āmantā|

yassa vā pana manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ aghānakānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| saghānakānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ manindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha ghānindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati| (ghānandriyamūlakaṃ)

337. (ka) yassa itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti? āmantā|

(kha) yassa vā pana purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ na itthīnaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| itthīnaṃ upapaj‍jantīnaṃ tāsaṃ purisindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha itthindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa  itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti? āmantā|

(kha) yassa vā pana jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa  itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ na itthīnaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| itthīnaṃ upapaj‍jantīnaṃ tāsaṃ jīvitindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha itthindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

yassa  itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa saumanassindriyaṃ…pai॰… upaikkhindriyaṃ…pai॰… saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ…pai॰… manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti? āmantā|

yassa vā pana manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ na itthīnaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| itthīnaṃ upapaj‍jantīnaṃ tāsaṃ manindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha itthindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati| (itthindriyamūlakaṃ)

338. (ka) yassa purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti? āmantā|

(kha) yassa vā pana jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ na purisānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| purisānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ jīvitindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha purisindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

yassa purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa saumanassindriyaṃ…pai॰… upaikkhindriyaṃ…pai॰… saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ…pai॰… manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti? āmantā|

yassa vā pana manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa  purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ na purisānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| purisānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ manindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha purisindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati| (purisindriyamūlakaṃ)

339. (ka) yassa jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti? āmantā|

(kha) yassa  vā pana saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| sabbaisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ saumanassindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha jīvitindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

yassa jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa upaikkhindriyaṃ…pai॰… saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ…pai॰… manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti? āmantā|

yassa vā pana manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha  tassa jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| sabbaisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ manindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha jīvitindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati| (jīvitindriyamūlakaṃ)

340. (ka) yassa saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti? āmantā|

(kha) yassa vā pana upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha  tassa saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai saumanassavippayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai niraudhasamāpan‍nānaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| saumanassaina upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai saumanassasampayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ upaikkhindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha saumanassindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

yassa saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ…pai॰… manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti? āmantā|

yassa  vā pana manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai saumanassavippayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai niraudhasamāpan‍nānaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| saumanassaina upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai saumanassasampayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ manindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha saumanassindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati| (saumanassindriyamūlakaṃ)

341. (ka) yassa upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa saddhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti? āmantā|

(kha) yassa vā pana saddhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai upaikkhāvippayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai niraudhasamāpan‍nānaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ saddhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| upaikkhāya upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai upaikkhāsampayuttacittassa  uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ saddhindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha upaikkhindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

yassa upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa pañ‍ñindriyaṃ…pai॰… manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti? āmantā|

yassa vā pana manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai upaikkhāvippayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai niraudhasamāpan‍nānaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ  upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| upaikkhāya upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai upaikkhāsampayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ manindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha upaikkhindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati| (upaikkhindriyamūlakaṃ)

342. (ka) yassa  saddhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa pañ‍ñindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti? āmantā|

(kha) yassa vā pana pañ‍ñindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa saddhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai saddhāvippayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai niraudhasamāpan‍nānaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ pañ‍ñindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ saddhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| sahaitukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai saddhāsampayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ pañ‍ñindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha saddhindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa saddhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti? āmantā|

(kha) yassa vā pana manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa  saddhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai saddhāvippayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai niraudhasamāpan‍nānaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ saddhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| sahaitukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai saddhāsampayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ manindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha saddhindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati| (saddhindriyamūlakaṃ)

343. (ka) yassa pañ‍ñindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti? āmantā|

(kha) yassa vā pana manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa pañ‍ñindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai ñāṇavippayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai niraudhasamāpan‍nānaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ pañ‍ñindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| ñāṇasampayuttānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai ñāṇasampayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ manindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha pañ‍ñindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati| (pañ‍ñindriyamūlakaṃ)

(kha) anulaumaaukāsau

344. yattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tattha sautindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti?…pai॰…|

(ga) anulaumapuggalaukāsā

345. (ka) yassa  yattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha sautindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti?

suddhāvāsaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha sautindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha| itaraisaṃ sacakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati sautindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha|

(kha) yassa  vā pana yattha sautindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha  tassa tattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

pañ‍cavaukārā cavantānaṃ acakkhukānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha sautindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| sacakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha sautindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa yattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti?

rūpāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha| sacakkhukānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati ghānindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

kāmāvacarā cavantānaṃ acakkhukānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| sacakkhukānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha ghānindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

yassa yattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha itthindriyaṃ…pai॰… purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti?

rūpāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ  tattha purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha| sacakkhukānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati purisindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha|

yassa vā pana yattha  purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

kāmāvacarā cavantānaṃ acakkhukānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| sacakkhukānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha purisindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa yattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti?

suddhāvāsaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha| itaraisaṃ sacakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati jīvitindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ acakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| sacakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha jīvitindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa  yattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti?

suddhāvāsaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha| itaraisaṃ sacakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati saumanassindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha|

(kha) yassa  vā pana yattha  saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

pañ‍cavaukārā cavantānaṃ acakkhukānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| sacakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha saumanassindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa yattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti?

suddhāvāsaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha| itaraisaṃ sacakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati upaikkhindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

pañ‍cavaukārā cavantānaṃ acakkhukānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ arūpānaṃ taisaṃ tattha upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| sacakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha upaikkhindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa yattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha saddhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti?

suddhāvāsaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha saddhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha| itaraisaṃ sacakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati saddhindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha saddhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha  tassa tattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

pañ‍cavaukārā cavantānaṃ acakkhukānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ arūpānaṃ taisaṃ tattha saddhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| sacakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha saddhindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

yassa yattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha pañ‍ñindriyaṃ…pai॰… manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti?

suddhāvāsaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ  tattha manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha| itaraisaṃ sacakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati manindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha|

yassa vā pana yattha manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

pañ‍cavaukārā cavantānaṃ acakkhukānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ arūpānaṃ taisaṃ tattha manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau  ca taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| sacakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha manindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati| (cakkhundriyamūlakaṃ)

346. yassa yattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha itthindriyaṃ…pai॰… purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti? āmantā|

yassa vā pana yattha purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

kāmāvacarā cavantānaṃ aghānakānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| saghānakānaṃ  upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha purisindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha ghānindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa yattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti? āmantā|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ aghānakānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| saghānakānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha jīvitindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha ghānindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa  yattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti? āmantā|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

kāmāvacarā cavantānaṃ aghānakānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ rūpāvacarānaṃ  taisaṃ tattha saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| saghānakānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha saumanassindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha ghānindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa yattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti? āmantā|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

kāmāvacarā cavantānaṃ aghānakānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ rūpāvacarānaṃ arūpāvacarānaṃ taisaṃ tattha upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| saghānakānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha upaikkhindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha ghānindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati| yassa yattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ…pai॰… manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti? āmantā|

yassa vā pana yattha manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

kāmāvacarā cavantānaṃ aghānakānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ rūpāvacarānaṃ arūpāvacarānaṃ taisaṃ tattha manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| saghānakānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha manindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha ghānindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati| (ghānindriyamūlakaṃ)

347. (ka) yassa yattha itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti? āmantā|

(kha) yassa  vā pana yattha purisindriyaṃ  uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

kāmāvacarā cavantānaṃ na itthīnaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| itthīnaṃ upapaj‍jantīnaṃ tāsaṃ tattha purisindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha itthindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa yattha itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti? āmantā|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

kāmāvacarā cavantānaṃ na itthīnaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ rūpāvacarānaṃ arūpāvacarānaṃ taisaṃ tattha jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| itthīnaṃ upapaj‍jantīnaṃ tāsaṃ tattha jīvitindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha itthindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa yattha itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti? āmantā|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

kāmāvacarā cavantānaṃ na itthīnaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ rūpāvacarānaṃ taisaṃ tattha saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| itthīnaṃ upapaj‍jantīnaṃ tāsaṃ tattha saumanassindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha itthindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

yassa yattha itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha upaikkhindriyaṃ…pai॰…  saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ…pai॰… manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti? āmantā|

yassa vā pana yattha manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

kāmāvacarā cavantānaṃ na itthīnaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ rūpāvacarānaṃ arūpāvacarānaṃ taisaṃ tattha manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha  itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| itthīnaṃ upapaj‍jantīnaṃ tāsaṃ tattha manindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha itthindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati| (itthindriyamūlakaṃ)

348. (ka) yassa yattha purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti? āmantā|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

kāmāvacarā cavantānaṃ na purisānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ rūpāvacarānaṃ arūpāvacarānaṃ taisaṃ tattha jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau  ca taisaṃ tattha purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| purisānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha jīvitindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha purisindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa  yattha purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti? āmantā|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

kāmāvacarā cavantānaṃ na purisānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ rūpāvacarānaṃ taisaṃ tattha saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| purisānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha saumanassindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha purisindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa yattha purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti? āmantā|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

kāmāvacarā cavantānaṃ na purisānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ rūpāvacarānaṃ arūpāvacarānaṃ taisaṃ tattha upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| purisānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha upaikkhindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha purisindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

yassa  yattha purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ…pai॰… manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti? āmantā|

yassa vā pana yattha manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

kāmāvacarā cavantānaṃ na purisānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ rūpāvacarānaṃ arūpāvacarānaṃ taisaṃ tattha manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| purisānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ  tattha manindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha purisindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati| (purisindriyamūlakaṃ)

349. (ka) yassa yattha jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti?

suddhāvāsānaṃ upapatticittassa uppādakkhaṇai asañ‍ñasattaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha| itaraisaṃ catuvaukāraṃ pañ‍cavaukāraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha jīvitindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati saumanassindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

catuvaukārā pañ‍cavaukārā cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| catuvaukāraṃ pañ‍cavaukāraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ  pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha saumanassindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha jīvitindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa yattha jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti?

suddhāvāsaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ asañ‍ñasattaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha| itaraisaṃ  catuvaukāraṃ pañ‍cavaukāraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha jīvitindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati upaikkhindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

catuvaukārā pañ‍cavaukārā cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| catuvaukāraṃ pañ‍cavaukāraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha upaikkhindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha jīvitindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

yassa yattha jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ…pai॰… manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti?

suddhāvāsaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ asañ‍ñasattaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha| itaraisaṃ catuvaukāraṃ pañ‍cavaukāraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha jīvitindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati manindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha|

yassa vā pana yattha manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

catuvaukārā pañ‍cavaukārā cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| catuvaukāraṃ pañ‍cavaukāraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha manindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha jīvitindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati| (jīvitindriyamūlakaṃ)

350. yassa yattha saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha upaikkhindriyaṃ…pai॰… saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ…pai॰… manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti? āmantā|

yassa vā pana yattha manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ  cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai saumanassavippayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha saumanassindriyaṃ  uppaj‍jati| saumanassaina upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai saumanassasampayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha manindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha saumanassindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati| (saumanassindriyamūlakaṃ)

351. (ka) yassa  yattha upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha saddhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti?

suddhāvāsaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha saddhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha| itaraisaṃ upaikkhāya upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai upaikkhāsampayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha upaikkhindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati saddhindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha saddhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai upaikkhāvippayuttacittassa  uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha saddhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| upaikkhāya upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai upaikkhāsampayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha saddhindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha upaikkhindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

yassa yattha upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha pañ‍ñindriyaṃ…pai॰… manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti?

suddhāvāsaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha| itaraisaṃ upaikkhāya upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai upaikkhāsampayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai  taisaṃ tattha upaikkhindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati manindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha|

yassa vā pana yattha manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai upaikkhāvippayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| upaikkhāya upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai upaikkhāsampayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha manindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha upaikkhindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati| (upaikkhindriyamūlakaṃ)

352. (ka) yassa yattha saddhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha pañ‍ñindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti?

suddhāvāsaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha saddhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha pañ‍ñindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha| itaraisaṃ sahaitukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai saddhāsampayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha saddhindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati pañ‍ñindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha pañ‍ñindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha saddhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai saddhāvippayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha pañ‍ñindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha saddhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| sahaitukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai saddhāsampayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha pañ‍ñindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha saddhindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa yattha saddhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha manindriyaṃ  uppaj‍jitthāti?

suddhāvāsaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha saddhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha manindriyaṃ  uppaj‍jittha| itaraisaṃ sahaitukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai saddhāsampayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha saddhindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati manindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha saddhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai saddhāvippayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha saddhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| sahaitukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai saddhāsampayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha  manindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha saddhindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati| (saddhindriyamūlakaṃ)

353. (ka) yassa yattha pañ‍ñindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jitthāti?

suddhāvāsaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha pañ‍ñindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha| itaraisaṃ ñāṇasampayuttānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai ñāṇasampayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha pañ‍ñindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati manindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha pañ‍ñindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai ñāṇavippayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha pañ‍ñindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| ñāṇasampayuttānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai ñāṇasampayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai  taisaṃ tattha manindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jittha pañ‍ñindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati| (pañ‍ñindriyamūlakaṃ)

(gha) pac‍canīkapuggalau

354. (ka) yassa cakkhundriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati tassa sautindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jitthāti? uppaj‍jittha|

(kha) yassa vā pana sautindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha tassa cakkhundriyaṃ na uppaj‍jatīti? natthi|

(ka) yassa cakkhundriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati tassa ghānindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jitthāti? uppaj‍jittha|

(kha) yassa vā pana ghānindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha tassa cakkhundriyaṃ na uppaj‍jatīti? natthi|

yassa cakkhundriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati tassa itthindriyaṃ…pai॰… purisindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jitthāti? uppaj‍jittha|

yassa vā pana purisindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha tassa cakkhundriyaṃ na uppaj‍jatīti? natthi|

(ka) yassa  cakkhundriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati tassa jīvitindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jitthāti? uppaj‍jittha|

(kha) yassa vā pana jīvitindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha tassa cakkhundriyaṃ na uppaj‍jatīti? natthi|

yassa  cakkhundriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati tassa saumanassindriyaṃ…pai॰… upaikkhindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jitthāti? uppaj‍jittha|

yassa vā pana upaikkhindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha tassa cakkhundriyaṃ na uppaj‍jatīti? natthi|

yassa cakkhundriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati tassa saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ…pai॰… manindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jitthāti? uppaj‍jittha|

yassa vā pana manindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha tassa cakkhundriyaṃ na  uppaj‍jatīti? natthi…pai॰…|

355. (ka) yassa pañ‍ñindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati tassa manindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jitthāti? uppaj‍jittha|

(kha) yassa vā pana manindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha tassa pañ‍ñindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jatīti? natthi|

(ṅa) pac‍canīkaaukāsau

356. yattha cakkhundriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati tattha sautindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jitthāti?…pai॰…|

(ca) pac‍canīkapuggalaukāsā

357. (ka) yassa yattha cakkhundriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati tassa tattha sautindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jitthāti?

pañ‍cavaukārā cavantānaṃ acakkhukānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ  tattha cakkhundriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha sautindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha| suddhāvāsai parinibbantānaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ arūpānaṃ taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jati sautindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jittha|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha sautindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha cakkhundriyaṃ na uppaj‍jatīti?

suddhāvāsaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha sautindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati| suddhāvāsai parinibbantānaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ arūpānaṃ taisaṃ tattha sautindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jittha cakkhundriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa yattha cakkhundriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati tassa tattha ghānindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jitthāti?

kāmāvacarā cavantānaṃ acakkhukānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha ghānindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha| rūpāvacarā cavantānaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ arūpānaṃ taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jati ghānindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jittha|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha ghānindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha cakkhundriyaṃ na uppaj‍jatīti?

rūpāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha ghānindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati| rūpāvacarā  cavantānaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ arūpānaṃ taisaṃ tattha ghānindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jittha cakkhundriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jati|

yassa yattha cakkhundriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati tassa tattha itthindriyaṃ…pai॰… purisindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jitthāti?

kāmāvacarā cavantānaṃ acakkhukānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha purisindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha| rūpāvacarā cavantānaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ arūpānaṃ taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jati purisindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jittha|

yassa  vā pana yattha purisindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha cakkhundriyaṃ na uppaj‍jatīti?

rūpāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha purisindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati| rūpāvacarā cavantānaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ arūpānaṃ taisaṃ tattha purisindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jittha cakkhundriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa yattha cakkhundriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati tassa tattha jīvitindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jitthāti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ acakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha jīvitindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha| suddhāvāsai parinibbantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jati jīvitindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jittha|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha jīvitindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha cakkhundriyaṃ na uppaj‍jatīti?

suddhāvāsaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha jīvitindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati| suddhāvāsai parinibbantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha jīvitindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jittha cakkhundriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa yattha cakkhundriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati tassa tattha saumanassindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jitthāti?

pañ‍cavaukārā cavantānaṃ acakkhukānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha saumanassindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha| suddhāvāsai parinibbantānaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ arūpānaṃ taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jati sautindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jittha|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha saumanassindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha cakkhundriyaṃ na uppaj‍jatīti?

suddhāvāsaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha saumanassindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati| suddhāvāsai parinibbantānaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ arūpānaṃ taisaṃ tattha saumanassindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jittha cakkhundriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa yattha cakkhundriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati tassa tattha upaikkhindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jitthāti?

pañ‍cavaukārā  cavantānaṃ acakkhukānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ arūpānaṃ  taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha upaikkhindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha| suddhāvāsai parinibbantānaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jati upaikkhindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jittha|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha upaikkhindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha cakkhundriyaṃ na uppaj‍jatīti?

suddhāvāsaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha upaikkhindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati| suddhāvāsai parinibbantānaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ tattha upaikkhindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jittha cakkhundriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jati|

yassa yattha cakkhundriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati tassa tattha saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ…pai॰… manindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jitthāti?

pañ‍cavaukārā cavantānaṃ acakkhukānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ arūpānaṃ taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha manindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha| suddhāvāsai parinibbantānaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jati manindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jittha|

yassa vā pana yattha manindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha cakkhundriyaṃ na uppaj‍jatīti?

suddhāvāsaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha manindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati| suddhāvāsai parinibbantānaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ tattha manindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jittha cakkhundriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jati| (cakkhundriyamūlakaṃ)

358. yassa yattha ghānindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati tassa tattha itthindriyaṃ…pai॰… purisindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jitthāti?

kāmāvacarā cavantānaṃ aghānakānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha ghānindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha purisindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha| rūpāvacarānaṃ arūpāvacarānaṃ taisaṃ tattha ghānindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jati purisindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jittha|

yassa vā pana yattha purisindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha ghānindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jatīti? āmantā|

(ka) yassa yattha ghānindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati tassa tattha jīvitindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jitthāti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ aghānakānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha ghānindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha jīvitindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha| suddhāvāsānaṃ taisaṃ tattha ghānindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jati jīvitindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jittha|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha jīvitindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha ghānindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jatīti? āmantā|

(ka) yassa yattha ghānindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati tassa tattha saumanassindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jitthāti?

kāmāvacarā cavantānaṃ aghānakānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ rūpāvacarānaṃ  taisaṃ tattha ghānindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha saumanassindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha| suddhāvāsānaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ arūpānaṃ taisaṃ tattha ghānindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jati saumanassindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jittha|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha saumanassindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha ghānindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jatīti? āmantā|

yassa yattha ghānindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati tassa tattha upaikkhindriyaṃ…pai॰… saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ…pai॰… manindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jitthāti?

kāmāvacarā cavantānaṃ aghānakānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ rūpāvacarānaṃ arūpāvacarānaṃ taisaṃ tattha ghānindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha manindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha| suddhāvāsānaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ tattha ghānindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jati manindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jittha| yassa vā pana  yattha manindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha ghānindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jatīti? āmantā| (ghānindriyamūlakaṃ)

359. (ka) yassa yattha itthindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati tassa tattha purisindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jitthāti?

kāmāvacarā cavantānaṃ na itthīnaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha itthindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha purisindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha| rūpāvacarānaṃ arūpāvacarānaṃ taisaṃ tattha itthindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jati purisindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jittha|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha purisindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha itthindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jatīti? āmantā|

(ka) yassa yattha itthindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati tassa tattha jīvitindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jitthāti?

kāmāvacarā cavantānaṃ na itthīnaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ rūpāvacarānaṃ arūpāvacarānaṃ taisaṃ tattha itthindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha jīvitindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha| suddhāvāsānaṃ taisaṃ tattha itthindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jati jīvitindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jittha|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha jīvitindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha itthindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jatīti? āmantā|

(ka) yassa yattha itthindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati tassa tattha saumanassindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jitthāti?

kāmāvacarā cavantānaṃ na itthīnaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ rūpāvacarānaṃ taisaṃ tattha itthindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha saumanassindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha| suddhāvāsānaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ arūpānaṃ taisaṃ tattha itthindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jati saumanassindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jittha|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha  saumanassindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha itthindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jatīti? āmantā|

yassa  yattha itthindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati tassa tattha upaikkhindriyaṃ…pai॰… saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ…pai॰… manindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jitthāti?

kāmāvacarā cavantānaṃ na itthīnaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ rūpāvacarānaṃ arūpāvacarānaṃ taisaṃ tattha itthindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha manindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha| suddhāvāsānaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ tattha itthindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jati manindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jittha|

yassa vā pana yattha manindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha itthindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jatīti? āmantā| (itthindriyamūlakaṃ)

360. (ka) yassa yattha purisindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati tassa tattha jīvitindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jitthāti?

kāmāvacarā cavantānaṃ na purisānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ rūpāvacarānaṃ arūpāvacarānaṃ taisaṃ tattha purisindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha jīvitindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha| suddhāvāsānaṃ taisaṃ tattha purisindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jati jīvitindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jittha|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha jīvitindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha purisindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jatīti? āmantā|

(ka) yassa yattha purisindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati tassa tattha saumanassindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jitthāti?

kāmāvacarā cavantānaṃ na purisānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ rūpāvacarānaṃ taisaṃ tattha purisindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha saumanassindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha| suddhāvāsānaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ arūpānaṃ taisaṃ tattha purisindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jati saumanassindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jittha|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha saumanassindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha purisindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jatīti? āmantā|

yassa yattha purisindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati tassa tattha upaikkhindriyaṃ…pai॰… saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ…pai॰… manindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jitthāti?

kāmāvacarā cavantānaṃ na purisānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ rūpāvacarānaṃ arūpāvacarānaṃ taisaṃ tattha purisindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ  tattha manindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha| suddhāvāsānaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ tattha purisindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jati manindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jittha|

yassa vā pana yattha manindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha purisindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jatīti? āmantā| (purisindriyamūlakaṃ)

361. (ka) yassa  yattha jīvitindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati tassa tattha saumanassindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jitthāti?

catuvaukārā pañ‍cavaukārā cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha jīvitindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha saumanassindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha| suddhāvāsānaṃ upapatticittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai asañ‍ñasattā cavantāna taisaṃ tattha jīvitindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jati saumanassindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jittha|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha saumanassindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha jīvitindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jatīti?

suddhāvāsānaṃ upapatticittassa uppādakkhaṇai asañ‍ñasattaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha saumanassindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha jīvitindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha| suddhāvāsānaṃ upapatticittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai asañ‍ñasattā cavantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha saumanassindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jittha jīvitindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jati|

yassa yattha jīvitindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati tassa tattha upaikkhindriyaṃ…pai॰… saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ…pai॰… manindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jitthāti?

catuvaukārā pañ‍cavaukārā cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha jīvitindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha manindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha| suddhāvāsānaṃ upapatticittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai asañ‍ñasattā cavantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha jīvitindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jati manindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jittha|

yassa  vā pana yattha manindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha jīvitindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jatīti?

suddhāvāsaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ asañ‍ñasattaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha manindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha jīvitindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati| suddhāvāsānaṃ upapatticittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai asañ‍ñasattā cavantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha manindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jittha jīvitindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jati| (jīvitindriyamūlakaṃ)

362. yassa yattha saumanassindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati tassa tattha upaikkhindriyaṃ…pai॰… saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ…pai॰… manindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jitthāti?

sabbaisaṃ cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai saumanassavippayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha saumanassindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha manindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha| suddhāvāsaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ tattha saumanassindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jati manindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jittha|

yassa vā pana yattha manindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha saumanassindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jatīti? āmantā| (saumanassindriyamūlakaṃ)

363. (ka) yassa yattha upaikkhindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati tassa tattha saddhindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jitthāti?

sabbaisaṃ cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai upaikkhāvippayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai  taisaṃ tattha upaikkhindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha saddhindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha| suddhāvāsānaṃ upapatticittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ tattha upaikkhindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jati saddhindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jittha|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha saddhindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha upaikkhindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jatīti?

suddhāvāsaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha saddhindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha, nau ca  taisaṃ tattha upaikkhindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati| suddhāvāsānaṃ upapatticittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ tattha saddhindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jittha upaikkhindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jati|

yassa yattha upaikkhindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati tassa tattha pañ‍ñindriyaṃ…pai॰… manindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jitthāti?

sabbaisaṃ cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai upaikkhāvippayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha upaikkhindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha manindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha| suddhāvāsānaṃ upapatticittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ tattha upaikkhindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jati manindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jittha|

yassa vā pana yattha manindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha upaikkhindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jatīti?

suddhāvāsaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha manindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha upaikkhindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati| suddhāvāsānaṃ upapatticittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ tattha manindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jittha upaikkhindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jati| (upaikkhindriyamūlakaṃ)

364. yassa yattha saddhindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati tassa tattha pañ‍ñindriyaṃ…pai॰… manindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jitthāti?

sabbaisaṃ cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai saddhāvippayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha saddhindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha manindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha| suddhāvāsānaṃ upapatticittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ tattha saddhindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jati manindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jittha|

yassa vā pana yattha manindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha saddhindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jatīti?

suddhāvāsaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha manindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha saddhindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati| suddhāvāsānaṃ upapatticittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ tattha manindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jittha saddhindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jati| (saddhindriyamūlakaṃ)

365. (ka) yassa  yattha pañ‍ñindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati tassa tattha manindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jitthāti?

sabbaisaṃ cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai ñāṇavippayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ tattha pañ‍ñindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha manindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha| suddhāvāsānaṃ  upapatticittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ tattha pañ‍ñindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jati manindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jittha|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha manindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha tassa tattha pañ‍ñindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jatīti?

suddhāvāsaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha manindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jittha, nau ca taisaṃ tattha pañ‍ñindriyaṃ na uppaj‍jati| suddhāvāsānaṃ upapatticittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ tattha manindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jittha pañ‍ñindriyañ‍ca na uppaj‍jati| (pañ‍ñindriyamūlakaṃ)

(5) pac‍cuppan‍nānāgatavārau

(ka) anulaumapuggalau

366. (ka) yassa cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa sautindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

pacchimabhavikānaṃ pañ‍cavaukāraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ yai ca arūpaṃ upapaj‍jitvā parinibbāyissanti taisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ sautindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati| itaraisaṃ sacakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati sautindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana sautindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ acakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ sautindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| sacakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ  taisaṃ sautindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

pacchimabhavikānaṃ pañ‍cavaukāraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ yai ca rūpāvacaraṃ arūpāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jitvā parinibbāyissanti taisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati| itaraisaṃ sacakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati ghānindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ acakkhukānaṃ  upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| sacakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ ghānindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

pacchimabhavikānaṃ pañ‍cavaukāraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ yai ca rūpāvacaraṃ arūpāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jitvā parinibbāyissanti yai ca purisā aitainaiva bhāvaina katici bhavai dassaitvā  parinibbāyissanti taisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati| itaraisaṃ sacakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati itthindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ acakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| sacakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ itthindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

yassa  cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti? (sadisaṃ )

(ka) yassa cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

pacchimabhavikānaṃ pañ‍cavaukāraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati| itaraisaṃ sacakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati jīvitindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ acakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| sacakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ jīvitindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

pacchimabhavikānaṃ pañ‍cavaukāraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ yai ca sacakkhukā upaikkhāya upapaj‍jitvā parinibbāyissanti taisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati| itaraisaṃ sacakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati saumanassindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana saumanassindriyaṃ  uppaj‍jissati tassa cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ acakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| sacakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ saumanassindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

pacchimabhavikānaṃ pañ‍cavaukāraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ yai ca sacakkhukā saumanassaina upapaj‍jitvā parinibbāyissanti taisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati| itaraisaṃ sacakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati upaikkhindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa  vā pana upaikkhindriyaṃ  uppaj‍jissati tassa cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ acakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| sacakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ upaikkhindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

yassa cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ…pai॰… manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

pacchimabhavikānaṃ pañ‍cavaukāraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati| itaraisaṃ sacakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati manindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

yassa vā pana manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ  acakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| sacakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ manindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati| (cakkhundriyamūlakaṃ)

367. (ka) yassa ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

pacchimabhavikānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ yai ca rūpāvacaraṃ arūpāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jitvā parinibbāyissanti yai ca purisā aitainaiva bhāvaina katici bhavai dassaitvā parinibbāyissanti taisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati| itaraisaṃ saghānakānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ ghānindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati itthindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ  cavantānaṃ aghānakānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| saghānakānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ itthindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati ghānindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

pacchimabhavikānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ yai ca rūpāvacaraṃ arūpāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jitvā parinibbāyissanti yā ca itthiyau aitainaiva bhāvaina katici bhavai dassaitvā parinibbāyissanti taisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati| itaraisaṃ saghānakānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ ghānindriyañ‍ca  uppaj‍jati purisindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ aghānakānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| saghānakānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ purisindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati ghānindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

pacchimabhavikānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati| itaraisaṃ saghānakānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ ghānindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati jīvitindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ aghānakānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| saghānakānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ  taisaṃ jīvitindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati ghānindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

pacchimabhavikānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ yai ca saghānakā upaikkhāya upapaj‍jitvā parinibbāyissanti taisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati| itaraisaṃ saghānakānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ ghānindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati saumanassindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana saumanassindriyaṃ  uppaj‍jissati tassa ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ aghānakānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| saghānakānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ saumanassindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati ghānindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

pacchimabhavikānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ yai ca saghānakā saumanassaina upapaj‍jitvā parinibbāyissanti taisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati| itaraisaṃ saghānakānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ ghānindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati upaikkhindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ aghānakānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| saghānakānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ upaikkhindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati ghānindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

yassa  ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ…pai॰…  manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

pacchimabhavikānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati| itaraisaṃ saghānakānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ ghānindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati manindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

yassa vā pana manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ aghānakānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| saghānakānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ manindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati ghānindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati| (ghānindriyamūlakaṃ)

368. (ka) yassa itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

pacchimabhavikānaṃ itthīnaṃ upapaj‍jantīnaṃ yā ca itthiyau rūpāvacaraṃ arūpāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jitvā parinibbāyissanti yā ca itthiyau aitainaiva bhāvaina katici bhavai dassaitvā parinibbāyissanti tāsaṃ upapaj‍jantīnaṃ tāsaṃ itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca tāsaṃ purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati| itarāsaṃ itthīnaṃ upapaj‍jantīnaṃ tāsaṃ itthindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati purisindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati  tassa itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ na itthīnaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| itthīnaṃ upapaj‍jantīnaṃ tāsaṃ purisindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati itthindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

pacchimabhavikānaṃ itthīnaṃ upapaj‍jantīnaṃ tāsaṃ itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau  ca tāsaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati| itarāsaṃ itthīnaṃ upapaj‍jantīnaṃ tāsaṃ itthindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati jīvitindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ na itthīnaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| itthīnaṃ upapaj‍jantīnaṃ tāsaṃ jīvitindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati itthindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

pacchimabhavikānaṃ itthīnaṃ upapaj‍jantīnaṃ yā ca itthiyau upaikkhāya upapaj‍jitvā parinibbāyissanti tāsaṃ upapaj‍jantīnaṃ tāsaṃ itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca tāsaṃ saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati| itarāsaṃ itthīnaṃ upapaj‍jantīnaṃ tāsaṃ itthindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati saumanassindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ  cavantānaṃ na itthīnaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| itthīnaṃ upapaj‍jantīnaṃ tāsaṃ saumanassindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati itthindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa  itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

pacchimabhavikānaṃ itthīnaṃ upapaj‍jantīnaṃ yā ca itthiyau saumanassaina upapaj‍jitvā parinibbāyissanti tāsaṃ upapaj‍jantīnaṃ tāsaṃ itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca tāsaṃ upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati| itarāsaṃ itthīnaṃ upapaj‍jantīnaṃ tāsaṃ itthindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati upaikkhindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ na itthīnaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| itthīnaṃ upapaj‍jantīnaṃ tāsaṃ upaikkhindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati itthindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

yassa itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ…pai॰…  manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

pacchimabhavikānaṃ itthīnaṃ upapaj‍jantīnaṃ tāsaṃ itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca tāsaṃ manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati| itarāsaṃ itthīnaṃ upapaj‍jantīnaṃ tāsaṃ itthindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati manindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

yassa vā pana manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ na itthīnaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| itthīnaṃ upapaj‍jantīnaṃ tāsaṃ manindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati itthindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati| (itthindriyamūlakaṃ)

369. (ka) yassa purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

pacchimabhavikānaṃ purisānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati| itaraisaṃ purisānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ purisindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati jīvitindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ na purisānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| purisānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ jīvitindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati purisindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa  purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

pacchimabhavikānaṃ purisānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ yai ca purisā upaikkhāya upapaj‍jitvā parinibbāyissanti taisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati| itaraisaṃ purisānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ  taisaṃ purisindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati saumanassindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, tassa purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ na  purisānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| purisānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ saumanassindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati purisindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

pacchimabhavikānaṃ purisānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ yai ca purisā saumanassaina upapaj‍jitvā parinibbāyissanti taisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati| itaraisaṃ purisānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ purisindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati upaikkhindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ na purisānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| purisānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ upaikkhindriyañ‍ca  uppaj‍jissati purisindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

yassa purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ…pai॰… manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

pacchimabhavikānaṃ purisānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati| itaraisaṃ purisānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ purisindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati manindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

yassa  vā pana manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ na purisānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| purisānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ manindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati purisindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati| (purisindriyamūlakaṃ)

370. (ka) yassa jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

pacchimacittassa uppādakkhaṇai yassa cittassa anantarā upaikkhāsampayuttapacchimacittaṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati| itaraisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ jīvitindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati saumanassindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| sabbaisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ saumanassindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati jīvitindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa  jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

pacchimacittassa uppādakkhaṇai yassa cittassa anantarā saumanassasampayuttapacchimacittaṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati| itaraisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ jīvitindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati upaikkhindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| sabbaisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ  upaikkhindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati jīvitindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

yassa jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ…pai॰… manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

pacchimacittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati| itaraisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ jīvitindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati manindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

yassa vā pana manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ pavattai cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai taisaṃ manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| sabbaisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ manindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati jīvitindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati| (jīvitindriyamūlakaṃ)

371. (ka) yassa saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

saumanassasampayuttapacchimacittassa uppādakkhaṇai yassa cittassa anantarā saumanassasampayuttapacchimacittaṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa cittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati| itaraisaṃ saumanassaina upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai saumanassasampayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ saumanassindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati upaikkhindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai saumanassavippayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai  niraudhasamāpan‍nānaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| saumanassaina upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai saumanassasampayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ upaikkhindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati saumanassindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

yassa  saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ…pai॰… manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

saumanassasampayuttapacchimacittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati| itaraisaṃ saumanassaina upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai saumanassasampayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ saumanassindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati manindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

yassa vā pana manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai saumanassavippayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai niraudhasamāpan‍nānaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| saumanassaina upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai saumanassasampayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ manindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati saumanassindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati| (saumanassindriyamūlakaṃ)

372. yassa upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ…pai॰… manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

upaikkhāsampayuttapacchimacittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati| itaraisaṃ upaikkhāya upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai upaikkhāsampayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ upaikkhindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati manindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

yassa vā pana manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai upaikkhāvippayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai niraudhasamāpan‍nānaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| upaikkhāya upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai upaikkhāsampayuttacittassa  uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ manindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati upaikkhindriyañ‍ca  uppaj‍jati| (upaikkhindriyamūlakaṃ)

373. yassa saddhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa pañ‍ñindriyaṃ…pai॰… manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

pacchimacittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ saddhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati| itaraisaṃ sahaitukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai saddhāsampayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ saddhindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati manindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

yassa vā pana manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa saddhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai saddhāvippayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai niraudhasamāpan‍nānaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ saddhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| sahaitukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai saddhāsampayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ manindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati saddhindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati| (saddhindriyamūlakaṃ)

374. (ka) yassa  pañ‍ñindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

pacchimacittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ pañ‍ñindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati| itaraisaṃ ñāṇasampayuttānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai ñāṇasampayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ pañ‍ñindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati manindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa pañ‍ñindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cittassa bhaṅgakkhaṇai ñāṇavippayuttacittassa uppādakkhaṇai niraudhasamāpan‍nānaṃ asañ‍ñasattānaṃ taisaṃ manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ pañ‍ñindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| ñāṇasampayuttānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ pavattai ñāṇasampayuttacittassa  uppādakkhaṇai taisaṃ manindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati pañ‍ñindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati| (pañ‍ñindriyamūlakaṃ)

(kha) anulaumaaukāsau

375. yattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tattha sautindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?…pai॰…|

(ga) anulaumapuggalaukāsā

376. (ka) yassa yattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha sautindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

pacchimabhavikānaṃ pañ‍cavaukāraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha sautindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati| itaraisaṃ sacakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati sautindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha sautindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa tattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

pañ‍cavaukārā cavantānaṃ acakkhukānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha sautindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| sacakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha sautindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa yattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

pacchimabhavikānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ rūpāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati| itaraisaṃ sacakkhukānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati ghānindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa  vā pana yattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa  tattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

kāmāvacarā cavantānaṃ acakkhukānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha  cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| sacakkhukānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha ghānindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa yattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

pacchimabhavikānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ rūpāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ yai ca purisā aitainaiva bhāvaina katici bhavai dassaitvā parinibbāyissanti taisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati| itaraisaṃ sacakkhukānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati itthindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa tattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

kāmāvacarā cavantānaṃ acakkhukānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| sacakkhukānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha itthindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa yattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

pacchimabhavikānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ rūpāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ yā ca itthiyau aitainaiva bhāvaina katici bhavai  dassaitvā parinibbāyissanti tāsaṃ upapaj‍jantīnaṃ taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati| itaraisaṃ sacakkhukānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ  upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati purisindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa tattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

kāmāvacarā cavantānaṃ acakkhukānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| sacakkhukānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha purisindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa yattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

pacchimabhavikānaṃ pañ‍cavaukāraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati| itaraisaṃ sacakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati jīvitindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa tattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ acakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| sacakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha jīvitindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa yattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha saumanassindriyaṃ  uppaj‍jissatīti?

pacchimabhavikānaṃ pañ‍cavaukāraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ yai ca sacakkhukā upaikkhāya upapaj‍jitvā parinibbāyissanti taisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati| itaraisaṃ sacakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati saumanassindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa  vā pana yattha saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa tattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

pañ‍cavaukārā cavantānaṃ acakkhukānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| sacakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha saumanassindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa yattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa tattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

pañ‍cavaukārā cavantānaṃ acakkhukānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ arūpānaṃ taisaṃ tattha upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau  ca taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| sacakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha upaikkhindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

yassa yattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰… pañ‍ñindriyaṃ…pai॰… manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

yassa vā pana yattha manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa tattha cakkhundriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

pañ‍cavaukārā cavantānaṃ acakkhukānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ arūpānaṃ taisaṃ tattha manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau  ca taisaṃ tattha cakkhundriyaṃ  uppaj‍jati| sacakkhukānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati cakkhundriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati| (cakkhundriyamūlakaṃ)

377. (ka) yassa  yattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

pacchimabhavikānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ yai ca purisā aitainaiva bhāvaina katici bhavai dassaitvā parinibbāyissanti taisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati| itaraisaṃ saghānakānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha ghānindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati itthindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa  tattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

kāmāvacarā cavantānaṃ aghānakānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| saghānakānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha itthindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati ghānindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa yattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

pacchimabhavikānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ yā ca itthiyau aitainaiva bhāvaina katici bhavai dassaitvā parinibbāyissanti tāsaṃ upapaj‍jantīnaṃ taisaṃ tattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati| itaraisaṃ saghānakānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha ghānindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati purisindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa tattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

kāmāvacarā cavantānaṃ aghānakānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| saghānakānaṃ  upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha purisindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati ghānindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa yattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

pacchimabhavikānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati| itaraisaṃ saghānakānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha ghānindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati jīvitindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa tattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

sabbaisaṃ cavantānaṃ aghānakānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha jīvitindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| saghānakānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha jīvitindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati ghānindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa yattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

pacchimabhavikānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ yai ca saghānakā upaikkhāya  upapaj‍jitvā parinibbāyissanti taisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati| itaraisaṃ saghānakānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ  taisaṃ tattha ghānindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati saumanassindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa tattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

kāmāvacarā cavantānaṃ aghānakānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ rūpāvacarānaṃ taisaṃ tattha saumanassindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| saghānakānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha saumanassindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati ghānindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

(ka) yassa  yattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

pacchimabhavikānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ yai ca saghānakā saumanassaina upapaj‍jitvā parinibbāyissanti taisaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati| itaraisaṃ saghānakānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha ghānindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati upaikkhindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa tattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

kāmāvacarā cavantānaṃ aghānakānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ rūpāvacarānaṃ arūpāvacarānaṃ taisaṃ tattha upaikkhindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| saghānakānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha upaikkhindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati ghānindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|

yassa yattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha saddhindriyaṃ…pai॰…  pañ‍ñindriyaṃ…pai॰… manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

pacchimabhavikānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati| itaraisaṃ saghānakānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha ghānindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati manindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

yassa vā pana yattha manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa tattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

kāmāvacarā cavantānaṃ aghānakānaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ rūpāvacarānaṃ arūpāvacarānaṃ taisaṃ tattha manindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha ghānindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| saghānakānaṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha manindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati ghānindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati| (ghānindriyamūlakaṃ)

378. (ka) yassa  yattha itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati tassa tattha purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissatīti?

pacchimabhavikānaṃ itthīnaṃ upapaj‍jantīnaṃ yā ca itthiyau aitainaiva bhāvaina  katici bhavai dassaitvā parinibbāyissanti tāsaṃ upapaj‍jantīnaṃ tāsaṃ tattha itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati, nau ca tāsaṃ tattha purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati| itarāsaṃ itthīnaṃ upapaj‍jantīnaṃ tāsaṃ tattha itthindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati purisindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati|

(kha) yassa vā pana yattha purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati tassa tattha itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jatīti?

kāmāvacarā cavantānaṃ na itthīnaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ upapaj‍jantānaṃ taisaṃ tattha purisindriyaṃ uppaj‍jissati, nau ca taisaṃ tattha itthindriyaṃ uppaj‍jati| itthīnaṃ upapaj‍jantīnaṃ tāsaṃ tattha purisindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jissati itthindriyañ‍ca uppaj‍jati|
